# ========================================================================
# HAND IDENTIFICATIONS -- P2600 on items that do not carry it yet.
# These lead the file: the Geni id is the FIRST edit on any individual,
# and a name item is not an exception to that.
# 167 in the three identification ledgers, 165 already on Wikidata, 30 a run beyond the ones this run touches.
# ========================================================================
Q141502958	P2600	"6000000004313804007"
Q141502959	P2600	"6000000001821187530"

# ========================================================================
# NAME ITEMS. One file, per the 2026-08-30 rule -- there is no
# longer a second batch to remember to run. They follow the Geni ids
# above, per the 2026-09-04 correction.
# ========================================================================
# Name items the Garborg batches need, AND the statements that use them.
#
# Each CREATE is followed by `Qperson  Pprop  LAST` for every bearer who
# ALREADY holds a QID -- LAST is exactly how you point at what was just
# created. A person this run is also CREATING cannot be linked here, because
# LAST would then name the person; they wait for the next run.
#
# A patronymic is its own item even where the spelling exists as a given
# name: CLAUDE.md, one name item per USAGE. Q141152710 Aadnesson is
# the pattern -- labels and P31.
#
# A created PATRONYMIC also carries P144 based on: the given names it derives
# from, one value for every such name the fathers who bear it actually carry.
# That is what the resolution algorithm reads -- a person gets P5056 when their
# father's P735 item is among these values -- so an item minted without them
# can never resolve anybody. reports/patronymic-items-to-create.tsv resolves
# them, and leaves out any given name whose label is ambiguous or has no item.

# Ingebretson -- patronymic, 4 bearer(s) in the batches
# create a new item
CREATE
#   the item just created: set the en label to "Ingebretson"
LAST	Len	"Ingebretson"
#   set the mul label to "Ingebretson"
LAST	Lmul	"Ingebretson"
#   set the ja label to "インゲブレートソン"
LAST	Lja	"インゲブレートソン"
#   set the zh label to "英厄布雷特松"
LAST	Lzh	"英厄布雷特松"
#   set the ko label to "이에브레트손"
LAST	Lko	"이에브레트손"
#   set the en description to "patronymic"
LAST	Den	"patronymic"
#   P31 instance of = Q110874 patronymic
LAST	P31	Q110874
#   P31 instance of = Q130444148
LAST	P31	Q130444148
#   P31 instance of = Q10673705
LAST	P31	Q10673705

LAST	P1705	mul:"Ingebretson"

LAST	P282	Q8229
#   P144 based on = Q30229695 Ingebret
LAST	P144	Q30229695

#   are limited per batch, and everything for one QID goes at once, in

#   capped -- a label added during item creation is good.
